    WF-16 — Study on the four-port birdcage¶

    Item briefs and execution history (operator directive 2026-10-01). The §7 row is the status of record.

    §9 item 244 — 2026-10-05 (chain U4; depends on item 240)¶

    Depends on item 240 landing on main with WF-15 ✅; if it has not, skip this item.

    Drafted by a general-purpose agent for the 2026-10-05 03:00 daily review; the review reviews it. Licence: §10 chain U4 (PROJECT_PLAN.md:4648–4650), a weekly-enumerated step (§9 rule (4)), serial on U3. No band, tolerance, Done-when or validation target moves. Every band below is imported by name from the file:line cited; none is set here.

    WF-16 step 1: the Study object on the F-small four-port birdcage. The Study class item 240 lands is extended, not duplicated. It builds item 192's ×1 fixture on exact port rectangles with κ off, solves it at 64 MHz, and returns S and a Touchstone file. Both must equal ANS-4 step 5's registered exact-rectangle records at 1e-9, with PORT-11's reciprocity / passivity / C4 gates imported and green. Its new public names are registered in WF-14's envelope. - Executor: implementer. It makes additive src/ changes (new classes in item 240's study.py, plus private sheet-geometry helpers copied verbatim from tests), adds __all__ exports, writes one new test module, and updates the registry and snapshot. No existing gate module, helper, record or src/ function is edited. - Tier: W0 standard (WF-15's W1, verbatim). W1 heavy by ceiling (-n 2, container 400 s, the ceiling items 192 / 225 gave the same fixture at the same width). W2 heavy (-n 8, 590 s). W3 / W4 standard (the WF-14 / WF-13 windows, verbatim). Complex build for W0–W2. The row's "heavy by prediction" stands. - Class: chain step (solve), measured median 11 min (2026-10-05 03:00 restock table). - Cost: ≈ 11 measured / ≈ 60 predicted min. That is 15 fixed + ≈ 30 code + windows ≈ 2 + 2.5 + 0.5 + 5 + 0.1 + 0.6 min + ≈ 4 spare. Tight against the 1-hour timebox. If W1 is not green by minute 40, park (rule (d)). - If item 240's landed API differs from its brief (names, the result object, the solve/accessor split), the executor adapts the names in this brief to what landed. The anchors, bands, records, ranks and windows below are not adapted. It discloses the mapping in the journal.

    Extension points U4 adds to item 240's API (names are proposals; the review or executor may rename them): - (E1) A four-port birdcage geometry object on exact port rectangles: FourPortBirdcageGeometry (frozen dataclass). - It has a f_small() classmethod that returns item 192's ×1 fixture. - .build(comm) returns the mesh, cell tags, the narrowed sheet facet tags, and one record per port (tag, azimuth, h, w = A/h, area, facet count). - The strip fraction is fixed at the SPEC's exact rectangle, so no ragged route is offered (operator ruling 2026-10-02). It takes no κ argument. - The conductor (σ = SIGMA_WIRE_S_PER_M) is a geometry field. The phantom is the Study's material slot. - (E2) A lumped-port drive/sweep excitation: LumpedPortDrive(z0_ohm=50.0, port_impedance_ohm=50.0, drive_voltage_v=1+0j). - It builds the four LumpedSheetPortSpecs and PortDefinitions from the geometry's port records, field for field as the direct call does. - It runs run_n_port_sparameter_sweep(..., degree=study.degree). - For this excitation the Study passes no Dirichlet field (zero tangential E). - (E3) An S + Touchstone result: the solve result additionally exposes s_matrix, z_matrix, sweep (the SParameterSweepResult), port_definitions and specs, plus .write_touchstone(path). - .write_touchstone(path) calls export_touchstone([sweep], port_definitions, output_path=path, write_csv_companion=False) on rank 0 only, then Barrier, and returns the path on every rank. - .mean_sar() raises on a port Study. - (E4) New public names FourPortBirdcageGeometry and LumpedPortDrive, exported in __all__ (item 240's 28 → 30). If item 240 exported its result class, the new attributes ride on it; otherwise nothing more is exported.

    Settled from the code (read before designing). - The records. ANS4_EXACT_STRIP_S_P1_RECORDS (tests/validation/test_ans4_exact_strip_records.py:141–226) is degree 1, κ off, ×1 (117 206 cells). - It was registered from 20261003T093408Z_ANS-4-item192-W1-p1.log:2117–2132 and is asserted at -n 2 (ANS4_EXACT_STRIP_RECORD_RANK_WIDTH, :227). - In its own module it is held to STEP3A_RECORD_RTOL = 1e-6 (test_ans4_resolution_ladder.py:198). - ANS4_EXACT_STRIP_S_P2_X1 (:233–318) is the degree-2 record, measured at -n 8 (20261003T094036Z_ANS-4-item192-W3-p2.log:2199–2216). It is "printed comparand only, never asserted" (:229–232). - A degree-2 record exists, but only at -n 8. There is no degree-2 record at -n 2, and none is invented. The FEM_EM_ANS4_EXACT_DEGREE knob has no default (:13–14, :336–343): unset, the module skips. - The 1e-9 band is LEG_D0_REPRODUCTION_BAND (tests/validation/test_port_birdcage_four_port.py:165). It is this fixture family's print-precision reproduction band (:131–135). known-issues.md:80 names it as the tightest record band on a MUMPS solve in tests/. Imported, not restated. - Why 1e-9 is backed across processes. MUMPS is not bitwise across processes at -n 2 (known-issues.md:69, OPS-43: 1-ULP drift in 2 of 34 children). - The same comparison (fresh solve against the 10-digit record, Frobenius-relative) read 9.557e-11 at 64 MHz in two separate processes: 20261004T093239Z_ANS-4-item212-W0-p1-unset.log:2172 and 20261004T183107Z_ANS-4-item225-W0-p1-unset.log:2172. That is ≈ 10× under the band. - Against full-precision JSON the cross-process spread is 5.628e-16 / 8.256e-16 at degree 1, -n 2 (…item212-W0…:2179, …item225-W0…:2179). It is 8.912e-13 at degree 2, -n 8 (…item192-W3-p2.log:2300). - So the record term is print rounding. A .9e print is ≤ 5e-10 relative per entry, hence ≤ 5e-10 in the Frobenius norm, by construction. Rounding plus solve spread is ≤ 5.01e-10 < 1e-9 at both degrees. - At degree 2 this is reasoned, not yet measured: P2_X1 has never been asserted against a fresh process. - The direct call is the exact-strip module's base rung, verbatim (:442–448): _four_port_rung(name, np.zeros(4), frequency_hz=FREQUENCY_64_HZ, degree=d, leg_sheet_strip_fraction=GATED_WIDTH_FRACTION). - Where things are defined: test_port_birdcage_leg_offset_sweep.py:199–555; FREQUENCY_64_HZ at test_lossy_sphere_fullwave.py:94; GATED_WIDTH_FRACTION = 0.5 at test_port_lumped_narrowed_sheet.py:132. - It returns mesh, cell_tags, facet_tags, problem, specs, port_defs, s, z, sheets, cells (:511–555). The Study is compared against those. - What the direct call composes, all of which (E1)/(E2) must reproduce: - Mesh: _build (tests/mesh/test_birdcage_leg_offset.py:107–171) → MeshGenerator.birdcage_port_domain (src/fem_em_solver/io/mesh.py:3975) with the module constants. - Sheets: _sheet_areas (:228–238, via src _interface_facet_tags, io/mesh.py:290), _sheet_azimuth_deg (:209), _projected_extents (:186), _port_frame / _narrowed_radial (leg_offset_sweep.py:151–196), _sheet_facet_count (tests/mesh/test_two_torus_port_sheet.py) and _facet_group_area (tests/mesh/test_two_torus_port_facets.py). - Problem: leg_offset_sweep.py:357–371. Background HomogeneousMaterial(0, 1, 1); material_map {CONDUCTOR_CELL_TAG 1: σ 800, PHANTOM_CELL_TAG 3: SALINE_SIGMA 0.5 / SALINE_EPSILON_R 78}; "pec_zero_tangential_a". - Specs: :373–392, with interior=True, drive_direction=(0, 0, 1) and no κ. - Port definitions: :394–405, orientation="leg_gap_axial_plus_z", negative tag 1. - Sweep: :442–448. - Constants: test_birdcage_port_tags.py:22–32, test_birdcage_leg_gaps.py:66, test_birdcage_port_sheet_prerequisite.py:52, test_birdcage_port_sheets.py:70–77, test_port_birdcage_lumped_column.py:100–101, test_port_gap_voltage_impedance.py:235, test_lossy_sphere_fullwave.py:92–93, test_port_package_sparameters.py:140. - src/ cannot import any of these (WF-13's rule), so (E1) copies the helpers verbatim, in operation order, into a private src/ module, and f_small() restates the constants. Anchor (K) pins both. - The phantom slot. GelledSalinePhantomMaterial(SALINE_SIGMA, SALINE_EPSILON_R, FREQUENCY_64_HZ) goes in as phantom_material with the default phantom_tag 3, which equals PHANTOM_CELL_TAG. The conductor stays in material_map. - build_material_fields writes float(phantom_material.sigma) (core/time_harmonic.py:285–286) exactly as material_map writes float(tagged_material.sigma) (:254–255). The DG0 arrays are therefore bitwise equal by construction, and (U) checks it. - A tag in both maps raises (:262–265). The material frequency must equal the problem's (:462–465). The phantom-cell check is reduced across ranks (:277), so an empty rank at -n 8 is safe. - Touchstone. - export_touchstone (src/fem_em_solver/ports/touchstone.py:111) writes .12e pairs (:75) and the frequency at .9e (:84), row-major for N ≥ 3 (:88–94). - It writes a generated_utc line (:177), so file bytes are never reproducible: compare numbers, never bytes. - It refuses placeholder sweeps (:141–149; the flag is set at ports/sparameters.py:556). - The file is read by load_touchstone (:204) and by the pinned skrf (tests/environment/test_scikit_rf_version.py). - PORT-11's gates are as the exact-strip module imports them (test_g, :684–714): - (i) ‖S − Sᵀ‖/‖S‖ ≤ RECIPROCITY_BAND 1e-3 (test_port_lumped_sheet_sweep.py:86); - (ii) σ_max(S) and every column power ≤ 1 + PASSIVITY_SIGMA_TOLERANCE 1e-9 (test_port_birdcage_four_port.py:129); - (iii′) every Z-class spread ≤ ADJACENT_SPREAD_BAND 0.005 (test_port_birdcage_lumped_column.py:121), and pooled / worst ≥ POOLED_SEPARATION_FLOOR 10 (test_port_birdcage_four_port.py:173). - Helpers: _circulant_classes and _class_spread (test_port_birdcage_four_port.py:176). - Expected at 64 MHz, degree 2, -n 8: 2.662e-15, σ_max 0.999819887372, spreads 0.0333 / 0.0338 / 0.0441 %, separation 957.45× (…item192-W3-p2.log:2264). - Expected at degree 1, -n 2: 4.509e-16, 0.999760093664, 0.0747 / 0.0691 / 0.0897 %, 451.71× (…item225-W0-p1-unset.log:2160).

    Anchors (asserted). - (K) Constants and refusals, structural (no solve). - Every field of FourPortBirdcageGeometry.f_small() == the imported test constant it restates. - A κ keyword raises TypeError, and a ragged (None) strip fraction raises ValueError. - Study(...).degree == 2 without the argument. Predicted ≤ 1e-12.

    Negative controls (rule (e)). - (c1) Cross-degree record swap, asserted. The (R) comparator, applied to the Study's S against the other degree's record, must exceed LEG_D0_REPRODUCTION_BAND. - Backing: …item192-W3-p2.log:2223. The p2-vs-p1 exact 64 MHz class means move 6.67 / 2.54 / 4.52 %, the same two records on the same fixture. - The norm-relative distance between the two registered records, by hand arithmetic from :170–197 and :262–289, is ≈ 4.9 %, ≈ 4.9e7× the band. Print the in-test value; it must reproduce ≈ 0.049 to two digits, otherwise the comparator is mis-wired: stop. - (c2) Port transposition, asserted on (T2)'s comparator. Swap P1 ↔ P2 (rows and columns) of the loaded Touchstone matrix and compare it against the record. It must exceed LEG_D0_REPRODUCTION_BAND. - The swap carries the adjacent class into opposite slots, eight entries off by |adj − opp|. By hand arithmetic on the records it reads ≈ 0.254 (P2_X1["64"]) or ≈ 0.249 (P1["64"]), ≈ 2.5e8× the band. - Backing: the registered record itself. The control is a deterministic function of it, computed in-test and printed before it is asserted. The review may relabel it as predicted. - Why not PORT-7's transpose control: S here is reciprocal to ≤ 2.7e-15, so S against Sᵀ is blind (test_touchstone_skrf_crosscheck.py:21–22 says so for reciprocal records). A cyclic port rotation is also near-blind under C4 (it moves S by about the class spreads, ≈ 4e-4). Both are printed, not asserted. - (c3) 1-ULP σ through the Study's phantom slot, asserted by construction. Use GelledSalinePhantomMaterial(np.nextafter(0.5, 1), 78, f). (U)'s DG0 compare must read exactly 2⁻⁵³ = 1.1102230246251565e-16 on > 0 owned dofs (globally reduced). There is no solve. - Backing: the assignment copies the scalar (core/time_harmonic.py:285), the ulp of 0.5 is 2⁻⁵³, and MAT-1's (c1) measured that exact value on the same code line (20260930T003229Z_MAT-1-reanchor-W1.log:491; the sphere fixture, so the mechanism is shared and the fixture is not). - Ceiling: this is the smallest nonzero reading, so it shows that (U)'s == 0.0 is at full resolution.

    What the code says (read by this drafter on fed0e27c, not run). The brief's premise is stated precisely here because it differs from the shorter form in which it reached the review. - _study_solve_ports (study.py:609–676) calls cond_kwargs, cond_terms = _conductor_setup(self, built, comm) at :625, maps the conductor tag only on the solid route (material_map=({} if geom.as_hole else {…}), :632–636), passes **cond_kwargs to TimeHarmonicProblem (:640: facet_tags= the outer-box meshtags, pec_facet_tags=(OUTER_BOX_TAG,)), passes extra_bilinear_terms=cond_terms to the sweep only when set (:651), and stores conductor_terms / conductor_facet_tags on the result (:673–674). - _study_solve_port_fields (:1592–1660) does none of that: no _conductor_setup, an unconditional material_map={geom.conductor_tag: …} (:1619–1623), no conductor keywords, no extra_bilinear_terms, no conductor_* fields on its result. - _conductor_setup (:1877–1902) refuses a non-LeontovichWall conductor (TypeError) and a LeontovichWall on as_hole=False (ValueError, "as_hole") before it touches built. - TimeHarmonicProblem validates every material_map tag against the MPI-reduced cell-tag set (core/time_harmonic.py:150–181, collective). - So the defect has two faces, and neither is "silently Leontovich-less fields on the hole route": - (F1) Hole route (as_hole=True, conductor LeontovichWall() or None): Study.sar / b1_plus / b1_plus_at raise ValueError: material_map references tags that do not exist in problem.cell_tags: [1], because the hole mesh has no tag-1 cells. Same message, same 80 798-cell build, measured once before: step 7's first W1, when step 7's own copy of these lines still mapped tag 1 (20261008T190040Z_WF-16-s7-W1.log:947, known tags [2, 3, 101, 102, 103, 104, 201, 202, 203, 204]). Study.solve() on the same Study works (step 7, 20261010T171026Z_OPS-70-item360-W9-copper.log:1824–1825). Loud, not silent: the copper Study's SAR and |B₁⁺| outputs have simply never run. - (F2) Solid route + LeontovichWall() (as_hole=False): Study.sar / b1_plus silently return the σ = 800 S/m solid-conductor fields, while Study.solve() on the same Study refuses with _conductor_setup's ValueError. This is the silent face, and it is item 382's drafter's original wording. - The YAML front end (src/fem_em_solver/study_yaml.py) refuses conductor: kind: leontovich without geometry.as_hole: true at parse (_parse_conductor, :333–348), so it never reaches (F2); a conductor + outputs.sar / outputs.b1_plus file reaches (F1) through _sar_summary / _b1_plus_summary (:667–706, study._solve_port_fields at :679) and raises. No test covers that combination (tests/package/test_wf1_yaml_copper.py has no outputs; test_wf1_yaml_sar_b1.py has no conductor). - run_n_port_sparameter_sweep accepts keep_fields=True together with extra_bilinear_terms (ports/sparameters.py:310–327, the per-drive call at :450–461 passes both), so the fix needs no change below study.py.

    The fix (one function, copied lines, no shared helper). In _study_solve_port_fields, between msh = built["mesh"] and the TimeHarmonicProblem(...) call, and in the same order as _study_solve_ports: 1. cond_kwargs, cond_terms = _conductor_setup(self, built, comm) — after msh = built["mesh"], before the problem (:624–625's order; the no-mesh refusal node below depends on it). 2. material_map=({} if geom.as_hole else {geom.conductor_tag: HomogeneousMaterial(...)}) — :632–636 copied with its comment. 3. **cond_kwargs as the problem's last keyword (:640). 4.     Precondition check on fdd2d03f — what the API can and cannot build (drafter's reading of the code; nothing run). - The Study can put capacitor sheets on the PEC hole with no new src/ code. CapacitorSheets(c_f, esr_ohm=0.0, on=…, drive=LumpedPortDrive()) (src/fem_em_solver/study.py:690–731) dispatches to _study_solve_capacitor_sheets (:746–), which builds the same geometry and problem lines as the four-port route, including the hole route's empty material_map (:775–779). With conductor=None, _conductor_setup returns ({}, None) (:1972–1976): natural PEC on as_hole=True, exactly item 429's fixture. The capacitor sheets are the four leg-gap port sheets (P1–P4) of FourPortBirdcageGeometry — F-small has no separate ring-gap sheets — which is where TH-17's / Study.tune's C_mode lives (one capacitor per gap). Nothing is missing. - But no in-model 4×4 at C_mode exists, by construction of the API. CapacitorSheets requires on non-empty and refuses an on that leaves no port to drive (study.py:713–719, :764–765); Study.solve returns the reduced S over the kept ports (:789–790), so the largest in-model "tuned" matrix is 3×3 (one capacitor) and the PORT-22 construction is 1×1 (P1 kept, P2–P4 at C). The weekly's "the in-model 4×4 at C_mode" is therefore read as the in-model reduced networks at C_mode — Route A (on=("P2","P3","P4"), PORT-22's own construction, tests/validation/test_port22_driven_sweep_resonance.py:15–17) and the four per-sheet 3×3s (PORT-24 / item 373's construction) — each against reduce_terminated_ports(S₀, z0, …) (src/fem_em_solver/ports/circuit.py:81) on the same Study's untuned 4×4. That is PORT-22 (i)'s circuit = field identity on this fixture, as the weekly named it. - The "Im z₁ of the tuned matrix" clause is true by construction (rule (h) flag, for the review). The only 4×4 that carries a tuned z₁ is the circuit-side Z_t = Z(S₀) + z_cap·I₄, and diag(F·Z_t·Fᴴ)[1] = z₁ + 1/(jωC_mode) = Re z₁ + j(Im z₁ − Im z₁) — TH-17's own _loop_z(z₁, f, C) (tests/validation/test_th17_birdcage_eigenmodes.py:854–856, "zero at a resonance of the four-capacitor structure") evaluated at the C that tune defined from that same z₁ (study.py:1055). Its imaginary part is rounding (predicted ≤ 1e-13 × |z₁|). The field-side alternative does not separate the modes: Route A's Z_in(P1) + z_cap is ≈ 0.0500 − 5.1e-5j Ω at C_mode and ≈ 0.0120 + 5.8e-6j Ω at the k = 2 capacitance (drafter's unlogged circulant arithmetic on item 429's printed z_k, 20261014T004318Z_WF-16-s6-W1.log:945), because P1 couples to modes 1, 2 and 3 alike — so a field-side "Im ≈ 0" reading would let the k = 2 control pass, not miss. Pose: the clause is asserted as the weekly wrote it, on the circuit-side Z_t, and labelled a by-construction identity (a record, rule (h)) in the docstring, the node name and the journal; the field-side Route-A reading is printed beside it for both capacitances. The review may instead demote it to printed only. - The circuit = field identity holds at any C (it is a linear-network identity; item 373 measured it at C_F with r = 0.2 and 0). It is therefore not a tuning statement by itself; the k = 2 control below shows its comparator reads C, and the printed S_A(C_k2) reading shows the identity is C-agnostic.

    Review ruling 2026-10-15 03:00 (items 463, 472)¶

    • Item 463 (U52) accepted with the drafter's reading. CapacitorSheets must leave a port to drive, so "the in-model 4×4 at C_mode" is read as the in-model reduced networks — Route A (PORT-22's construction) and the four per-sheet 3×3s — against the circuit reduction of the same Study's 4×4 at REDUCTION_BAND. The "Im z₁ of the tuned matrix" clause is true by construction (z₁ + 1/(jωC_mode) is TH-17's _loop_z), so under rule (h) it is printed as a record, not asserted as a gate; the added wrong-C comparator (c2) shows the comparison reads C. No src/ change. WF-16 step 6 closes on it.
    • Item 472 (U53) accepted with its src/ addition (≈ 40 lines in study.py: lift the hole-route conductor refusal for the F-human LumpedPortDrive route, add a 32-port ring read on ports/ring_modes.py), disclosed under ruling (c), the pre-existing refusal gates re-run (RG1 test_wf16_study_tune.py, RG2 step 8 W0) and the registry nodes plus pages regenerated in the same slot (the WF-14 rule). The comparand 1.181185799070e-10 F is the value WF-12 step 3 printed (20261006T170222Z_WF-12-step3-a2-W1.log:6366) in a window that exited red on its own pre-registered (2); the weekly named it the record, and it is used as that record. If the src/ change and its re-gates have not passed by minute 30, park (rule (d)).

    Review ruling 2026-10-15 18:00 (item 472, the (K0) re-band)¶

    Ratified. (K0) is the W0 check that the ring read does not depend on port row order. Unshuffled and shuffled copies measured 1 ulp apart (1.1813757652308145e-10 against …148e-10, 20261015T213212Z_WF-16-s12-W0.log:63). s_to_z inverts the two row orders in different floating-point sequences. Both shuffled reads equal the closed form and ring_mode_rows at 0.000e+00 (:52). The brief's "bit for bit" was a drafting slip: a reordered linear solve has no such identity. The project's band for exact identities under rounding is EXACT_IDENTITY_RTOL. The executor re-registered the clause on the same quantity and disclosed it in the commit, §9, this file and the code comment. That is rule (f)'s path, and the review now ratifies it. No gate rests on the bitwise clause: (R0), (I), (a) and (c1) were each green at their pre-registered bands. Standing note for drafters: do not pre-register "bit for bit" on a quantity computed through a dense inverse or solve whose row order differs between the two sides. Use EXACT_IDENTITY_RTOL.

Item 993's W1a (20261115T210648Z_WF-16-U13a-W1-n1.log:102) read surface group 'port_1': fragment child 241 of 'PortSheet1' is not embedded in any volume. The cause is the solids, not the route. With emit_port_sheets=True, each port box is two halves, port_P<i>_lower / _upper. They share the full dx × dz sheet, so the CadQuery f = 0.5 strip lies on that interface. The f = 0.5 strip is then a face embedded inside one volume, which is the topology U12′ proved (tests/mesh/cq_author_geo34_step1d.py:19: a 0.8 × 0.6 face at z = 0.47 strictly inside the unit box). The config gains one port volume group per leg in place of 1a's verbatim halves.
